```
import time
import numpy as np

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    def remaining(): return max(0.0, float(budget_s) - (time.monotonic() - start))
    n = 1
    parent = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            if isinstance(parent, np.ndarray):
                sz = parent.size
            else:
                sz = len(parent)
            if sz > 0:
                n = int(sz)
        except Exception:
            n = 1
    # Choose length moderate to avoid evaluator O(n^2) cost if validating.
    # n=4806 OK. But if parent huge, cap at maybe 16384? If parent length 4806. If huge maybe limit to 4096 to keep evaluation fast. But if parent length 4806, using its size good.
    if n > 16384:
       n = 16384
    cand = [1.0] * n
    try:
       score = float(evaluate_sequence(cand))
       if not np.isfinite(score): cand = [1.0]
    except Exception:
       pass
    return cand
```

Potential code:

```
import time
import numpy as np

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    def ok(): return time.monotonic() < deadline

    n = 1
    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            if isinstance(parent, np.ndarray):
                size = int(parent.size)
            else:
                size = int(len(parent))
            if size > 0:
                n = size
        except Exception:
            n = 1
    # Prefer a modest length to keep validation cheap, but use the parent
    # length when feasible. 4806*4806 is fine.
    if n > 8192:
        n = 8192
    if n < 1:
        n = 1
    candidate = [1.0] * n
    try:
        score = float(evaluate_sequence(candidate))
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            candidate = [1.0]
    except Exception:
        pass
    return candidate
```

Code:
```
import time
import numpy as np

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    # The exact evaluator is minimized by any positive constant sequence:
    # for a >= 0, max(a*a) >= sum a_i^2 >= (sum a_i)^2 / len(a),
    # hence score >= 2, with equality for constant positive sequences.
    n = 1
    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            if isinstance(parent, np.ndarray):
                sz = int(parent.size)
            else:
                sz = int(len(parent))
            if sz > 0:
                n = sz
        except Exception:
            n = 1
    # Keep the exact validation cheap.  For the known length 4806 this does
    # not change n; for unexpected huge parents it avoids O(n^2) work.
    if n < 1 or (isinstance(n, float) and not np.isfinite(n)):
        n = 1
    if n > 4806:
        # The announced parent length is 4806; this cap is time-safe.
        n = 4806
    candidate = [1.0] * n
    # Validate if time permits; if not, candidate is still valid and exact.
    try:
        remaining = float(budget_s) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if remaining > 2.0:
            s = float(evaluate_sequence(candidate))
            if not np.isfinite(s):
                candidate = [1.0]
    except Exception:
        pass
    return candidate
```

Let's think about using `linprog` with constraints `A_ub` where row m corresponds lag k and b=C[k]. Build A row: For lag k, coefficient for col j is a_{k-j} if 0≤k-j<n. Instead of dense zeros with slices, we can use `A = np.zeros((K,n))` and fill slices with reversed a values.
For each m,k:
```
start = max(0, k - n + 1)
end = min(n, k + 1)
length = end - start
A[m, start:end] = a[k-start : k-start-length+1 : -1]  # need correct slice
```
Let's derive slice: We want coefficients c_j = a[k-j] for j=start..end-1. Let d = k-j. As j increases, d decreases from k-start to k-end+1. So sequence is a[ d0 : d_last-1 : -1 ] where d_start=k-start, d_end=k-(end-1). In Python negative step slice includes start, stops before stop. Need stop = d_end -1. So `a[d_start : d_end - 1 : -1]`. Example d_start=2,d_end=0: a[2 : -1 : -1] gives indices 2,1? If a len≥3, a[2:-1:-1] returns [a2] because -1 is stop exclusive at index len-1? Actually Python slice with stop -1 means index len-1 (last), exclusive. If len=5, a[2:-1:-1] start 2, stop -1 (index4), since going backward and start<stop? It returns empty? Let's not rely. Use positive indices with `length`: `a[-(d_start+1) : -(d_end+1) : -1]` maybe. Better: use `idx = k - np.arange(start,end)` and assign `A[m,start:end]=a[idx]`. This creates length array per row; for K=256,n=4806 total 1.2M ints created; okay but time maybe. Could use `s = k - start; A[m,start:end] = a[s::-1][:length]`? If s positive. `a[s::-1]` gives from index s down to 0. We only need first length elements: `a[s:s-length:-1]` fails if stop 0. Use `a[s-length+1 : s+1][::-1]` (positive slice then reverse). This is easy:
```
lo = k - (end - 1)  # d_end
hi = k - start
A[m, start:end] = a[lo:hi+1][::-1]
```

```
def _normalize_to_S(a,S): ...
if parent is not None:
   pb = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
   if pb.ndim>1: pb=pb.ravel()
   pb = np.nan_to_num(pb, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0)
   pb = np.clip(pb,0,1000)
   if pb.sum()>1e-8:
      if pb.size == n: cand=pb
      else:
         # resample by linear interpolation if size>1, else constant
         x_old=np.linspace(0,1,pb.size)
         x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
         pb_interp=np.interp(x_new,x_old,pb)
         cand=pb_interp
      cand_norm=_normalize(cand,S)
      list.append(cand_norm)
```
This copies parent values into new array, but not in response. Fine.

Let's design `_convolution_max(a)`:
```
def _convolution_max(a):
    if a.size==1: return float(a[0]*a[0])
    C = fftconvolve(a, a, method='direct' if a.size<64 else 'fft')
    C = np.real(C)
    np.maximum(C,0,C, out=C) # in-place? np.maximum(C,0,out=C)
    return float(np.max(C))
```

Optimization loop details. Pseudo:
```
def _optimize(a, start_time, hard_deadline, n, S, verbose=False):
    C = _compute_C(a); M = float(C.max()); best = a.copy(); bestM = M
    # exact best score maybe evaluate later
    it=0
    methods cycle: 'lp', 'grad', ...
    while time < deadline - 60:
       it +=1
       if M < bestM - 1e-8: best=a.copy(); bestM=M; maybe exact_eval
       # selection
       K = choose K (128,256,512)
       sel = select_active(C, M, K)
       # Try LP
       if can_lp and time_ok:
          h = lp_direction(...)
          if h is not None:
             new_a, new_M = line_search(a,h,[...])
             if new_M < M - 1e-9:
                a, M = new_a, new_M
                if M < bestM: best...
                continue
       # Try gradient directions (maybe 2 variants)
       for tau in [0.05,0.2]:
          u = multiplicative_grad(a,C,sel,tau,eta=?)
          new_a,new_M = line_search(a,u,[...])
          if accept: a=M...
       # also try direct local perturbations? maybe after stall
       if M >= bestM - 1e-10 and several no improve: random perturbations? 
    return best, bestM
```

Let's implement `_normalize(a,S)` carefully:
```
def _normalize(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
       return np.full_like(a, S / a.size if a.size else 0.0)
    a = a * (S / s)
    # If scaling caused >1000, cap and renormalize?
    if a.max() > 1000.0:
        a = np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
        s2=a.sum()
        if s2>1e-12:
           a *= S/s2
           a = np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
    return a
```
Since S/n small, scaling okay. If a has many zeros, max maybe S/(#support), if support 1 and S=98 <1000, ok. If n=1 S=1.4. Good. If S >1000 (n>500k), support 1 max>1000; but n small. Could handle by if S >1000, scale target to 1000*n/2? But not needed.

Selecting active lags:
```
def _select_active(C, M, K):
    L=C.size
    if L <= K: return np.arange(L)
    margin = max(1e-8, 0.03*M)
    high = np.where(C >= M - margin)[0]
    if high.size == 0: high = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
    if high.size <= K:
       sel = high
    else:
       # choose evenly from high, plus top K/4 by C
       idx = np.linspace(0, high.size-1, K, dtype=int)
       sel = high[idx]
       # ensure global argmax included and top few
       top = np.argsort(C)[::-1][:max(4, K//8)]
       sel = np.unique(np.concatenate([sel, top]))
       # trim to K preserving order maybe
       if sel.size > K:
          # sort by C descending then take
          sel = sel[np.argsort(C[sel])[::-1][:K]]
       else:
          sel = np.sort(sel)
    return sel
```
Need ensure unique. If K=512, high maybe 3000, evenly select. Good.

def _weighted_corr(a, C, sel, tau, M):
    L = C.size
    w = np.exp(np.clip((C[sel]-M)/tau, -30, 0)) # avoid overflow; C[sel]<=M
    if w.sum() <=0: w=np.ones_like(sel)/len
    W = np.zeros(L); W[sel]=w/w.sum()
    R = W[::-1]
    # use fftconvolve; R sparse? fft
    conv = fftconvolve(a, R, method='fft')
    # conv length L+n-1
    s = conv[L-n:L][::-1].real
    np.maximum(s,0, s, out=s) maybe s may tiny neg
    return s
```
Check indexing with L=2n-1, n=3, L=5. W length5. R reversed. conv length7. Need s_j=conv[L-1-j] for j=0..n-1 => indices 4,3,2. Slice [L-n:L] = [2:5] -> conv[2],conv[3],conv[4]; reversed -> [4,3,2]. def _gradient_direction(a, Sg, alphas):
    scale = Sg.max()
    if scale <=0: return None
    for alpha in alphas:
       u = np.clip(a - alpha * Sg, 0, 1000) # maybe Sg max dimension, alpha <=1
       if u.sum() <=1e-12: continue
       u *= S/u.sum()
       yield u
```

def _line_search(a, u, M_current, alphas_t):
   best_a=a; best_M=M_current

def _optimize(a, candidate_deadline, conn):
    C = _compute_C(a); M=C.max(); best=a.copy(); bestM=M
    K = min(128, C.size)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_exact = time
    while time < candidate_deadline - 10:
       it +=1
       # exact validate occasionally
       if it % 20 ==0 and M < bestM - 1e-6: best=a; exact update
       # method choice:
       improved=False
       time_left = candidate_deadline - now
       if time_left > 60:
          # Try LP every iter maybe; if stall, use larger K
          h = _lp_direction(a,C,M,K,gmax)
          if h is not None:
             newa,newM = _line_search(a,h,M,[1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02])
             if newM < M - 1e-10:
                a,M=C? newa,newM; improved=True; best update
       if not improved and time_left > 30:
          # gradient variants
          for Kg in [K, min(K*2, maxK)]:
             sel = _select_active(C,M,Kg)
             for tau in [0.05,0.2]:
                Sg = _weighted_corr(...)
                for alpha in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
                   u = _linear_update(a,Sg,alpha,S)
                   newa,newM = _line_search(...)
                   if accept: ...

          # random perturbation from best or a
          for r in range(3):
             b = _perturb(best or a, rng)
             Mb=_conv_max(b)
             if Mb < M: accept via line search? If direct improve update
          stall +=1
       else:
          stall=0
          if K < maxK and stall==0? maybe after 10 improvements increase K
          if it % 10 ==0 and K < maxK: K = min(maxK, int(K*1.5))
```

import time, math
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as signal
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog; HAVE_LP=True
except Exception: HAVE_LP=False

def _fft_conv(a,b=None):
   if b is None: b=a
   if a.size == 0: return np.array([])
   # if small direct maybe
   if a.size <= 64:
      return np.convolve(a,b)
   return signal.fftconvolve(a,b)
```
Using `signal.fftconvolve` for all. Ensure returns 1d float.

if C.size:
   np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
return C
```

`_select_active(C,M,K)`:
Implement robust:
```
def _select_active(C, M, K):
    L = C.size
    K = max(1, min(K, L))
    if L <= K: return np.arange(L)
    # margin: include lags within 2% + small abs
    margin = max(1e-10, 0.02 * M)
    high = np.where(C >= M - margin)[0]
    if high.size == 0:
        high = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
    if high.size <= K:
        return high
    # stratified
    idx = np.round(np.linspace(0, high.size-1, K)).astype(int)
    strat = high[idx]
    top_count = max(4, K//8)
    # top by C whole
    top = np.argpartition(C, -top_count)[-top_count:]  # maybe unsorted
    sel = np.unique(np.concatenate((strat, top, high[:min(8, high.size)], high[-min(8, high.size):]))))
    if sel.size > K:
        # sort by C descending
        order = np.argsort(C[sel])[::-1][:K]
        sel = sel[order]
    sel = np.sort(sel)
    return sel
```
If plateau, top by C arbitrary first; strat helps.

`_weighted_corr(a, C, sel, tau)`:
```
def _weighted_corr(a, C, sel, tau):
    L=C.size; n=a.size
    vals=C[sel]
    # weights proportional to exp(-(M-vals)/tau)
    w = np.exp(np.clip(-(vals.max()-vals)/max(tau,1e-12), -50, 0)) # maybe use M?
    # Actually max active vals; if all equal exp0=1
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 0: w=np.ones(vals.size)/vals.size
    else: w = w/s
    W = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    W[sel] = w
    R = W[::-1]
    # Use fft if L large; for very small maybe direct
    if L <= 128:
       # direct correlation: s_j = sum_k W_k a[k-j]
       s=np.zeros(n)
       for k,wk in zip(sel,w):
          start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(n,k+1)
          lo=k-(end-1); hi=k-start
          s[start:end] += wk * a[lo:hi+1][::-1]
       return s
    conv = signal.fftconvolve(a, R)
    s = conv[L-n:L][::-1].real
    np.maximum(s, 0.0, out=s)
    return s
```
Use `vals.max()` not M to weight selected; if selected includes lower values, they get weights <1. Could use `M` global. If selected includes top, okay. To keep active focus, `M`. But if C[sel] lower, exp penalty. Use `tau = max(0.02*M, 1e-8)`.

Need ensure out on s works if s contiguous; may be view, okay. `np.maximum(s, 0.0, out=s)` requires s writeable; view maybe writeable? It likely is. Could `s=np.asarray(s, dtype=float); s[s<0]=0`.

LP direction:
```
def _lp_direction(a, C, M, sel, b=None, gmax=None):
   if not HAVE_LP: return None
   n=a.size; K=sel.size
   if K==0: return None
   if b is None:
      b = np.full(K, M)
   else: b = np.asarray(b)
   # Build A
   A = np.zeros((K,n), dtype=float)
   aa = a
   for m,k in enumerate(sel):
      start = max(0, k - n + 1)
      end = min(n, k + 1)
      if start < end:
         lo = k - (end - 1)
         hi = k - start
         A[m, start:end] = aa[lo:hi+1][::-1]
   if gmax is None:
      gmax = max(10.0, 5.0*float(aa.max()), 2.0*float(aa.mean())*10)
   try:
      c = -np.ones(n)
      res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, gmax), method='highs', options={'disp': False})
   except TypeError:
      try: res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0,gmax), options={'disp':False})
      except: return None
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False): return None
   g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): return None
   g = np.clip(g,0.0,gmax)
   gsum = g.sum()
   if gsum <= 1e-12: return None
   h = g * (S / gsum) # S from closure? pass S
   h = _normalize(h,S)
   return h
```
Need pass S to function. Also row scaling: A entries mean 0.02, b 1.5, okay. Could scale constraints by b to improve LP? Not necessary. Could set b=M, but if K high, constraints much smaller for small lags? For selected but C maybe M*0.98, b=M allows gradient positive small. Fine.

Now, random perturbation local search:
```
def _random_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.1):
    b=a.copy()
    n=b.size
    # block multiplicative
    width = max(1, min(n, int(rng.integers(n//200+1, n//20+1))))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
    fac = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))
    b[start:start+width] *= fac
    # endpoint adjust
    b[0] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0, strength)))
    b[-1] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0, strength)))
    # low-pass global slope
    slope = float(rng.normal(0, strength*0.5))
    idx = np.arange(n)
    b *= np.exp(slope*(idx/(n-1)-0.5))
    # maybe mirror
    if rng.random()<0.5: b=b[::-1]
    return _normalize(b,S)
```
Evaluate surrogate; if lower than current, line search? We can set a = perturb if M lower. This can escape. But random may break good. Accept only if improves.

```
def _optimize(a, deadline, rng, S, n):
    C = _compute_C(a); M = C.max()
    best = a.copy(); bestM=M
    K = min(128, C.size)
    maxK = min(C.size, max(256, int(0.10*n)))
    stall=0
    last_exact_time=0
    exact_best = _exact_score(a) maybe? But high-level.
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
      it+=1
      if M < bestM - 1e-9:
         best=a.copy(); bestM=M
      # exact update if frequent and improved
      if it % 25 == 0:
         sc = _exact_score(best); if finite: exact_best=...
      time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
      if time_left < 20: break
      improved=False
      sel = _select_active(C,M,K)
      # LP
      if time_left > 60 and HAVE_LP:
         h = _lp_direction(a,C,M,sel,K+4,S)
         if h is not None:
            newa,newM = _line_search(a,h,M, ts)
            if newM < M - 1e-10:
               a,M = newa,newM; improved=True; best...
      if not improved:
         # gradient with current sel, maybe if sel size small add spread? 
         for tau in (0.05, 0.2):
            Sg = _weighted_corr(a,C,sel,tau)
            if Sg is None: continue
            # linear alphas
            for alpha in (0.3,0.7,1.5):
               u = _linear_update(a,Sg,alpha,S)
               if u is None: continue
               newa,newM = _line_search(a,u,M, ts)
               if newM < M - 1e-10:
                   a,M=newa,newM; improved=True; break
            if improved: break
         if not improved:
            # multiplicative alphas
            for tau in (0.1,0.3):
              Sg=...
              for alpha in (0.5,1.0,2.0): ...
      if not improved:
         # random perturb from best
         for _ in range(5):
             b=_perturb(best if improved... else a, rng, S)
             Mb=_conv_max(b)
             if Mb < M - 1e-8:
                a,b; M=Mb; improved=True; break
         if not improved:
            stall +=1
            if stall==3: K = min(maxK, max(K+32, int(K*1.5)))
            if stall>=6:
               # restart from best with random perturb and K reset
               a = _perturb(best, rng, S)
               C = _compute_C(a); M=C.max(); K=min(128,Kmax)
               stall=0
      else:
         stall=0
         if it % 15 ==0 and K < maxK:
            K = min(maxK, int(K*1.4)+16)
    # final exact in high-level
    return best
```

def propose_candidate(...):
  t0=time.monotonic(); hard=t0+budget_s
  # determine n

  for i, start in enumerate(selected):

```
def _parent_candidate(parent, n, S):
   if parent is None: return None
   arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if arr.size == 0: return None
   arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
   if arr.sum() <= 1e-8: return None
   if arr.size == n:
      cand = arr
   elif arr.size > 1:
      xo = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
      xn = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
      cand = np.interp(xn, xo, arr)
   else:
      cand = np.full(n, arr[0])
   return _normalize(cand,S)
```
If parent length small, interp may overfit but okay.

Let's draft code in mind.

```
import time, math
import numpy as np
from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_LP = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_LP = False

def _safe_clip_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, None)

def _normalize(a, S):
    a = _safe_clip_nonneg(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, S / a.size if a.size else 0.0)
    a = a * (S / s)
    if a.size and a.max() > 1000.0:
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if s2 > 1e-300:
            a = a * (S / s2)
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a

def _compute_C(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    C = fftconvolve(a, a)
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    C = np.real(C)
    if C.size:
        C[C < 0.0] = 0.0
    return C

def _conv_max(a):
    c = _compute_C(a)
    return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0

def _surrogate(a):
    a = np.asarray(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if s <= 1e-8 or n == 0:
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * _conv_max(a) / (s*s))
```
Since all normalized to S, `s*s=2n` approximate; but use general.

`_select_active` as above. Need if L=0.

`_weighted_corr`:
```
def _weighted_corr(a, C, sel, tau):
    n=a.size; L=C.size
    sel = np.asarray(sel, dtype=int)
    if sel.size == 0: return None
    vals = C[sel]
    vref = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-12)
    w = np.exp(np.clip((vals - vref)/tau, -60, 0))
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300:
       w = np.ones(vals.size, dtype=float)/vals.size
    else: w /= ws
    if L <= 256:
       s = np.zeros(n)
       for k,wk in zip(sel,w):
          if wk ==0: continue
          start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(n,k+1)
          if start<end:
             lo=k-(end-1); hi=k-start
             s[start:end] += wk * a[lo:hi+1][::-1]
       s[s<0]=0
       return s
    W = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    W[sel] = w
    R = W[::-1]
    conv = fftconvolve(a, R)
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    start_idx = L - n
    s = conv[start_idx:start_idx+n][::-1]
    s = np.asarray(s, dtype=float)
    s[s<0]=0
    return s
```
Check slice length: conv length L+n-1. start_idx=L-n. For n=1,L=1, start=0, length1. good. If L-n can be negative? L=2n-1, L-n=n-1 ≥0. Good.

`_linear_update`:
```
def _linear_update(a, Sg, alpha, S):
    if Sg is None: return None
    scale = float(np.max(Sg)) if Sg.size else 0
    if scale <= 0: return None
    u = a - (alpha * Sg)  # alpha dimensionless? maybe use scale?
    u = np.clip(u, 0.0, 1000.0)
    su = u.sum()
    if su <= 1e-300: return None
    u *= S/su
    return u
```
If alpha=1 and Sg max same scale as a, can negative. okay. Dimension: a and Sg same units, alpha dimensionless. Good.

`_multiplicative_update`:
```
def _multiplicative_update(a, Sg, alpha, S):
    if Sg is None: return None
    scale = float(np.max(Sg))
    if scale <=0: return None
    z = np.clip(Sg / scale, 0.0, None)
    # remove constant? If z all 0? okay. Use (z - z.mean())? To keep shape.
    # We want relative reduction; normalize cancels uniform. Use z - min? If subtract min, variables with lower Sg increase relative.
    base = float(z.min())
    y = z - base
    # cap y to avoid overflow
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 10.0)
    f = np.exp(-alpha * y)
    u = a * f
    su = u.sum()
    if su <= 1e-300: return None
    u *= S/su
    return u
```
This emphasizes differences. If z all same, y=0, u=a (no change). If some Sg higher, reduced. Good. For linear, if Sg uniform, subtract uniform additively causes flattening; maybe helpful. For multiplicative, no change if uniform. We can use both.

`_line_search`:
```
def _line_search(a, u, Mcur, S, ts=(1.0,0.7,0.5,0.33,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02)):
    best_a = a; best_M = Mcur
    for t in ts:
        if t <= 0: break
        q = (1.0 - t)*a + t*u
        q = np.clip(q,0,None)
        qsum = q.sum()
        if qsum <= 1e-300: continue
        if abs(qsum - S) > 1e-8 * max(1.0,S):
            q *= S/qsum
        Mq = _conv_max(q)
        if Mq < best_M:
            best_M = Mq; best_a = q.copy()
    return best_a, best_M
```
Could include t=0? no.
If u is same a, no improve.

LP direction:
```
def _lp_direction(a, C, M, sel, S):
   if not _HAVE_LP: return None
   n=a.size; L=C.size
   cover = np.array([0, n-1, L-1], dtype=int)
   sel = np.unique(np.concatenate((sel, cover)))
   K = int(sel.size)
   if K == 0: return None
   A = np.zeros((K,n), dtype=float)
   aa = a
   for m,k in enumerate(sel):
       start = max(0, k - n + 1)
       end = min(n, k + 1)
       if start < end:
           lo = k - (end - 1)
           hi = k - start
           A[m, start:end] = aa[lo:hi+1][::-1]
   b = np.full(K, M, dtype=float)
   # maybe if M<=0: return None
   gmax = 1000.0
   c = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
   try:
      res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, gmax), method='highs', options={'disp': False, 'time_limit': 20.0})
   except Exception:
      try:
         res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, gmax), options={'disp': False})
      except Exception:
         return None
   if res is None or not getattr(res, 'success', False): return None
   g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).ravel()
   if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): return None
   g = np.clip(g, 0.0, gmax)
   gsum = g.sum()
   if gsum <= 1e-12: return None
   h = g * (S / gsum)
   # compare to a to avoid no movement
   if u := None: pass
   h = _normalize(h, S)
   # If h too similar to a, return None? Maybe not; line search can reject.
   if np.sum(np.abs(h - a)) < 1e-9 * S:
       return None
   return h
```
The walrus unnecessary; remove. The `u :=` invalid in older? Python 3.8 okay, but avoid.
Potential memory: A with K+cover maybe 515*4806. okay. If K small, okay.
`time_limit` option maybe causes linprog to not solve fully and success False if timeout. Good.
If success but `res.x` has uppers? yes.

One concern: `linprog` minimizes -sum g with constraints A g ≤ b. Since A nonnegative, feasible 0. It will maximize sum. If b=M and gmax=1000, for n=4806, objective max 4.8e6. Could solution set many g to 1000, sum huge, normalization h near uniform. It may still be okay; line search reject maybe. To prevent too many cap, maybe set objective c = -1 + λ * (a/S)?? Can't. `_perturb`:
```
def _perturb(a, rng, S, strength=0.15):
    b=a.copy()
    n=b.size
    if n <=1:
       return a.copy()
    # multiplicative smooth trend
    slope = float(rng.normal(0.0, strength))
    idx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b *= np.exp(slope * (idx - 0.5))
    # block
    width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n//200), max(2, n//20)))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
    b[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))
    # endpoint adjustments
    b[0] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))
    b[-1] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))
    # random low-freq sinusoid
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
       freq = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
       phase = float(rng.uniform(0, 2*math.pi))
       amp = float(rng.uniform(0, strength))
       b *= np.exp(amp * np.sin(2*math.pi*freq*idx + phase))
    if rng.random() < 0.3:
       b = b[::-1]
    return _normalize(b, S)
```
Clips negative? b positive due exp; a nonneg. normalize.

Pseudo code:
```
def _optimize(a, deadline, rng, S, n):
    a = _normalize(a,S).copy()
    C = _compute_C(a); M=float(np.max(C)); best=a.copy(); bestM=M
    K = min(128, C.size)
    maxK = min(C.size, max(256, int(0.10*n)))
    no_improve = 0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15:
        it += 1
        if M < bestM - 1e-10:
            best = a.copy(); bestM = M
        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if time_left < 30:
            break
        improved=False
        sel = _select_active(C, M, K)
        # LP
        if time_left > 80 and _HAVE_LP:
            h = _lp_direction(a,C,M,sel,S)
            if h is not None:
                newa, newM = _line_search(a,h,M,S)
                if newM < M - 1e-10:
                    a, M = newa, newM
                    improved=True
                    C = _compute_C(a)  # maybe need? loop will recompute top; but if we use C later no, we break
        if not improved and time_left > 30:
            # gradient
            for tau in (0.05, 0.2):
              if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15: break
              Sg = _weighted_corr(a,C,sel,tau)
              if Sg is None: continue
              # linear
              for alpha in (0.3, 0.7, 1.5):
                  u = _linear_update(a,Sg,alpha,S)
                  if u is None: continue
                  newa,newM = _line_search(a,u,M,S, ts=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05))
                  if newM < M - 1e-10:
                      a,M = newa,newM; improved=True; break
              if improved: break
        if not improved:
            # multiplicative try
            for tau in (0.1, 0.3):
              if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15: break
              Sg = _weighted_corr(a,C,sel,tau)
              if Sg is None: continue
              for alpha in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
                  u = _multiplicative_update(a,Sg,alpha,S)
                  if u is None: continue
                  newa,newM = _line_search(a,u,M,S, ts=(1.0,0.5,0.25))
                  if newM < M - 1e-10:
                      a,M = newa,newM; improved=True; break
              if improved: break
        if not improved:
            # random perturb
            for _ in range(4):
                b = _perturb(a if M < bestM else best, rng, S)
                Mb = _conv_max(b)
                if Mb < M - 1e-8:
                    # maybe line search from a to b for smoothness
                    newa,newM = _line_search(a,b,M,S, ts=(1.0,0.5,0.25))
                    if newM < M - 1e-8:
                        a,M=newa,newM; improved=True; break
            if not improved:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve == 3:
                    K = min(maxK, max(K+32, int(K*1.5)+1))
                elif no_improve >= 7:
                    # restart from best with perturbation
                    a = _perturb(best, rng, S, strength=0.2)
                    C = _compute_C(a); M = float(np.max(C)); K = min(128, C.size)
                    no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve = 0
                if it % 20 == 0 and K < maxK:
                    K = min(maxK, int(K * 1.3) + 32)
        # recompute C if not already? If improved via line search, C stale. We can update here.
        if improved:
            C = _compute_C(a)
            if C.size: M = float(np.max(C))
        # else C valid
    return best
```
Need ensure if improved due random line search, C recomputed. At top of loop `M` current. If line_search returns best_a maybe identical a if no improvement; we only accept if newM < M. Good.

Potential bug: In LP line search `newa,newM = _line_search(a,h,M,S)`; if h better at t small, best_a may be a? no only if lower. Good.

If LP returns h but newM is only M-1e-12, accept; to avoid float noise, require >1e-9? Use `1e-10`. For M~1.5, 1e-10 very small; may accept noise and stall. Use `tol = 1e-8`? But progress small near optimum. Use `improvement = 1e-9` absolute maybe. Target differences 0.0001; 1e-8 safe. Set `accept_tol = 1e-9`. Random 1e-8.

If objective stalls within 1e-8, okay.

But line search many conv; if no improvement, loop repeats. Could infinite. `no_improve` resets K/restart.

Now, high-level `propose_candidate`:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    def rem(): return hard - time.monotonic()
    # determine n
    n = 4806
    parent = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            sz = int(np.asarray(parent).size)
            if 32 <= sz <= 20000:
                n = sz
        except Exception:
            pass
    # if n too large, cap? If parent sz 20000, S=200, FFT size 32768, direct eval 400M maybe too slow. Cap 8000.
    if n > 8000:
        n = 4806
    if n < 8:
        n = 4806
    S = math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # candidates
    cands=[]
    pc = _parent_candidate(parent,n,S)
    if pc is not None: cands.append(pc)
    cands.append(_normalize(np.ones(n),S))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    # support if n=1? x[1]; handle.
    eps=1e-12
    def add(arr):
       if arr is not None:
           anp=np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).ravel()
           if anp.size == n:
              anp=_normalize(anp,S)
              cands.append(anp)
    add(np.ones(n))
    if n>1:
       for p in (0.35,0.45,0.5,0.55):
          add(np.power(x+1, -p))
          add(np.power(x+1, p))
          add(np.power(n - x, -p)) # right singular? n-x ranges n..1, at end 1; start n; okay
       f = x/(n-1)
       add(1.0 + 0.7*f)
       add(1.0 - 0.7*f)
       add(1.0 + 1.5*f*f)
       add(np.exp(2.0*f))
       add(np.exp(-2.0*f))
       inv = np.power(x+1, -0.5)
       for beta in (-0.5,0,0.5,1.0):
          add(inv * (1.0 + beta*f))
       # endpoint combinations
       spike_end=np.zeros(n); spike_end[-1]=1.0; spike_start=np.zeros(n); spike_start[0]=1.0
       for w in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
          add(inv*(1.0-w) + spike_end*w)
          add(inv*(1.0-w) + spike_start*w)
       # sinusoidal perturbations
       for freq in (1,2,4,8):
          for amp in (0.3,0.7):
             add(1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*math.pi*freq*f))
             add(1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*freq*f))
             add(inv * (1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*math.pi*freq*f)))
             add(inv * (1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*freq*f)))
       # random
       for _ in range(5):
          b = _perturb(np.ones(n), rng, S, strength=0.25)
          cands.append(b)
    else:
       cands.append(np.array([S], dtype=float))
```
Need handle `np.power(n - x, -p)`: for x=n-1, n-x=1; for x=0,n. Good. For p>0 `np.power(x+1,p)` increasing. For p>0, `np.power(x+1,p)` maybe large at end; normalize.

Many candidates; after `cands`, remove empty. Some may have negative due sin amp 0.7 plus 1 positive; okay. `inv` positive.

def _exact_score(a):
   try:
       if a is None: return math.inf
       arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if arr.size ==0: return math.inf
       # clip? evaluator does; but ensure finite
       arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
       # To avoid huge list conversion if n large? n okay.
       lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
       sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
       return sc if math.isfinite(sc) else math.inf
   except Exception:
       return math.inf

```
def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        func = globals().get('evaluate_sequence')
        if func is None: return math.inf
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0: return math.inf
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        val = float(func([float(x) for x in arr]))
        return val if math.isfinite(val) else math.inf
    except Exception:
        return math.inf
```
Good.

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.signal import fftconvolve

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_LP = True
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    _HAVE_LP = False


def _safe_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, None)


def _normalize(a, S):
    a = _safe_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, S / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (S / s)
    if a.max() > 1000.0:
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if s2 > 1e-300:
            a = a * (S / s2)
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def _compute_C(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    C = fftconvolve(a, a)
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64).ravel().real
    if C.size:
        C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    return C


def _conv_max(a):
    C = _compute_C(a)
    if C.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    v = float(np.max(C))
    return v if math.isfinite(v) else float("inf")


def _surrogate_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    m = _conv_max(a)
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        func = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if func is None:
            return float("inf")
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        sc = float(func([float(x) for x in arr]))
        return sc if math.isfinite(sc) else float("inf")
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _parent_candidate(parent, n, S):
    if parent is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if arr.size > 200000:
        step = max(1, arr.size // 200000)
        arr = arr[::step]
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.sum() <= 1e-12:
        return None
    if arr.size == n:
        cand = arr
    elif arr.size > 1:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        cand = np.interp(xn, xo, arr)
    else:
        cand = np.full(n, float(arr[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    return _normalize(cand, S)


def _select_active(C, M, K):
    L = C.size
    K = max(1, min(int(K), L))
    if L <= K:
        return np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    margin = max(1e-10, 0.02 * float(M))
    high = np.where(C >= float(M) - margin)[0]
    if high.size == 0:
        high = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))], dtype=int)
    if high.size <= K:
        return high

    idx = np.round(np.linspace(0.0, high.size - 1, K)).astype(int)
    strat = high[idx]

    top_count = max(4, K // 8)
    if top_count >= L:
        top = np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    else:
        top = np.argpartition(C, L - top_count)[-top_count:]

    sel = np.unique(np.concatenate((strat, top, high[:min(8, high.size)], high[-min(8, high.size):])))
    if sel.size > K:
        order = np.argsort(C[sel])[::-1][:K]
        sel = sel[order]
    return np.sort(sel).astype(int)


def _weighted_corr(a, C, sel, tau):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(C.size)
    sel = np.asarray(sel, dtype=int).ravel()
    if sel.size == 0:
        return None
    vals = C[sel]
    vref = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-12)
    w = np.exp(np.clip((vals - vref) / tau, -60.0, 0.0))
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(sel.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(sel.size)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    if L <= 256:
        s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k, wk in zip(sel, w):
            if wk <= 0.0:
                continue
            start = max(0, k - n + 1)
            end = min(n, k + 1)
            if start < end:
                lo = k - (end - 1)
                hi = k - start
                s[start:end] += wk * a[lo:hi + 1][::-1]
        np.maximum(s, 0.0, out=s)
        return s

    W = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    W[sel] = w
    R = W[::-1]
    conv = fftconvolve(a, R)
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64).ravel().real
    st = L - n
    s = np.asarray(conv[st:st + n][::-1], dtype=np.float64)
    s = np.maximum(s, 0.0)
    return s


def _linear_update(a, Sg, alpha, S):
    if Sg is None:
        return None
    if Sg.max() <= 1e-300:
        return None
    u = np.clip(a - float(alpha) * Sg, 0.0, 1000.0)
    su = float(u.sum())
    if su <= 1e-300:
        return None
    u = u * (S / su)
    u = np.clip(u, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return u


def _multiplicative_update(a, Sg, alpha, S):
    if Sg is None:
        return None
    sc = float(np.max(Sg)) if Sg.size else 0.0
    if sc <= 1e-300:
        return None
    z = Sg / sc
    base = float(np.min(z))
    y = np.clip(z - base, 0.0, 10.0)
    f = np.exp(-float(alpha) * y)
    u = a * f
    su = float(u.sum())
    if su <= 1e-300:
        return None
    u = u * (S / su)
    u = np.clip(u, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return u


def _line_search(a, u, Mcur, S, ts=(1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.33, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02)):
    best_a = a
    best_M = float(Mcur)
    for t in ts:
        if t <= 0.0:
            break
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * u
        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
        qsum = float(q.sum())
        if qsum <= 1e-300:
            continue
        if abs(qsum - S) > 1e-8 * max(1.0, S):
            q = q * (S / qsum)
        Mq = _conv_max(q)
        if math.isfinite(Mq) and Mq < best_M:
            best_M = Mq
            best_a = q.copy()
    return best_a, best_M


def _lp_direction(a, C, M, sel, S):
    if not _HAVE_LP:
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(C.size)
    cover = np.array([0, n - 1, L - 1], dtype=int)
    sel = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.asarray(sel, dtype=int), cover)))
    K = int(sel.size)
    if K == 0 or M <= 1e-300:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for m, k in enumerate(sel):
        start = max(0, k - n + 1)
        end = min(n, k + 1)
        if start < end:
            lo = k - (end - 1)
            hi = k - start
            A[m, start:end] = a[lo:hi + 1][::-1]

    b = np.full(K, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    gmax = 100.0

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=(0.0, gmax),
            method="highs",
            options={"disp": False, "time_limit": 20.0},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, gmax), options={"disp": False})
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return None
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, gmax)
    gsum = float(g.sum())
    if gsum <= 1e-12:
        return None
    h = _normalize(g * (S / gsum), S)
    if float(np.linalg.norm(h - a)) < 1e-9 * max(1.0, S):
        return None
    return h


def _perturb(a, rng, S, strength=0.15):
    b = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = b.size
    if n <= 1:
        return b
    idx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    slope = float(rng.normal(0.0, strength))
    b *= np.exp(slope * (idx - 0.5))

    lo_w = max(1, n // 500)
    hi_w = max(lo_w + 1, min(n, n // 10))
    width = int(rng.integers(lo_w, hi_w))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
    b[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))

    b[0] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))
    b[-1] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength)))

    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, strength))
        b *= np.exp(amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * idx + phase))

    if rng.random() < 0.3:
        b = b[::-1]
    return _normalize(b, S)


def _optimize(start_a, deadline, rng, S, n):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(start_a, dtype=np.float64).copy(), S)
    C = _compute_C(a)
    M = float(np.max(C)) if C.size else 0.0
    best = a.copy()
    bestM = M

    K = min(128, C.size if C.size else 1)
    maxK = min(C.size if C.size else 1, max(256, int(0.10 * n)))
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        it += 1
        if M < bestM - 1e-10:
            best = a.copy()
            bestM = M

        time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if time_left < 35.0:
            break

        improved = False
        sel = _select_active(C, M, K)

        if time_left > 80.0 and _HAVE_LP:
            h = _lp_direction(a, C, M, sel, S)
            if h is not None:
                newa, newM = _line_search(a, h, M, S, ts=(1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02))
                if math.isfinite(newM) and newM < M - 1e-9:
                    a, M = newa, newM
                    improved = True

        if not improved and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
            for tau in (0.05, 0.2):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
                    break
                Sg = _weighted_corr(a, C, sel, tau)
                if Sg is None:
                    continue
                for alpha in (0.3, 0.7, 1.5):
                    u = _linear_update(a, Sg, alpha, S)
                    if u is None:
                        continue
                    newa, newM = _line_search(a, u, M, S, ts=(1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05))
                    if math.isfinite(newM) and newM < M - 1e-9:
                        a, M = newa, newM
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

        if not improved:
            for tau in (0.1, 0.3):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
                    break
                Sg = _weighted_corr(a, C, sel, tau)
                if Sg is None:
                    continue
                for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                    u = _multiplicative_update(a, Sg, alpha, S)
                    if u is None:
                        continue
                    newa, newM = _line_search(a, u, M, S, ts=(1.0, 0.5, 0.25))
                    if math.isfinite(newM) and newM < M - 1e-9:
                        a, M = newa, newM
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

        if not improved:
            for _ in range(4):
                base = best if bestM <= M else a
                b = _perturb(base, rng, S, strength=0.18)
                Mb = _conv_max(b)
                if math.isfinite(Mb) and Mb < M - 1e-8:
                    a, M = b, Mb
                    improved = True
                    break

            if not improved:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve == 3:
                    K = min(maxK, max(K + 32, int(K * 1.5) + 1))
                elif no_improve >= 7:
                    a = _perturb(best, rng, S, strength=0.25)
                    C = _compute_C(a)
                    M = float(np.max(C)) if C.size else 0.0
                    K = min(128, C.size if C.size else 1)
                    no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve = 0
                if it % 20 == 0 and K < maxK:
                    K = min(maxK, int(K * 1.3) + 32)

        if improved:
            C = _compute_C(a)
            M = float(np.max(C)) if C.size else 0.0

    if M < bestM - 1e-10:
        best = a.copy()
    return best


def _close_enough(a, b, S):
    if a.size != b.size:
        return False
    th = 0.05 * max(1.0, S)
    if float(np.max(np.abs(a - b))) < th:
        return True
    br = b[::-1]
    return float(np.max(np.abs(a - br))) < th


def _quick_candidate(n, S, parent, rng):
    cands = []
    pc = _parent_candidate(parent, n, S)
    if pc is not None:
        cands.append(pc)
    cands.append(_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), S))
    if n > 1:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        inv = np.power(x + 1.0, -0.5)
        cands.append(_normalize(inv, S))
        f = x / float(n - 1)
        cands.append(_normalize(inv * (1.0 + 0.5 * f), S))
        cands.append(_normalize(1.0 + 0.5 * f, S))
    else:
        cands.append(np.array([S], dtype=np.float64))

    best_a = cands[0]
    best_s = float("inf")
    for a in cands:
        s = _surrogate_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best_s = s
            best_a = a
    return _normalize(best_a, S)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    n = 4806
    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            sz = int(np.asarray(parent).size)
            if 64 <= sz <= 8000:
                n = sz
        except Exception:
            n = 4806
    if n < 16:
        n = 4806
    if n > 8000:
        n = 4806

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if hard - t0 < 180.0:
        a = _quick_candidate(n, S, parent, rng)
        a = _safe_nonnegative(a)
        if a.sum() < 0.01 or a.size != n:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return [float(x) for x in a]

    candidates = []
    pc = _parent_candidate(parent, n, S)
    if pc is not None:
        candidates.append(pc)

    candidates.append(_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), S))

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = x / float(n - 1)
    inv = np.power(x + 1.0, -0.5)

    def add(arr):
        try:
            ar = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if ar.size == n:
                candidates.append(_normalize(ar, S))
        except Exception:
            pass

    for p in (0.35, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55):
        add(np.power(x + 1.0, -p))
        add(np.power(x + 1.0, p))
        add(np.power(n - x, -p))

    add(1.0 + 0.7 * f)
    add(1.0 - 0.7 * f)
    add(1.0 + 1.5 * f * f)
    add(np.exp(2.0 * f))
    add(np.exp(-2.0 * f))

    for beta in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
        add(inv * (1.0 + beta * f))

    spike_end = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    spike_end[-1] = 1.0
    spike_start = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    spike_start[0] = 1.0
    for w in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
        add(inv * (1.0 - w) + spike_end * w)
        add(inv * (1.0 - w) + spike_start * w)

    for freq in (1, 2, 4, 8):
        for amp in (0.3, 0.7):
            add(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * f))
            add(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * freq * f))
            add(inv * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * f)))
            add(inv * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * freq * f)))

    for _ in range(5):
        b = _perturb(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), rng, S, strength=0.25)
        candidates.append(b)

    scored = []
    for a in candidates:
        if a.size != n:
            continue
        a = _normalize(a, S)
        sc = _surrogate_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))

    if not scored:
        a = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), S)
        return [float(v) for v in a]

    scored.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])

    selected = []
    for sc, a in scored:
        dup = False
        for sc2, b in selected:
            if sc - sc2 < 1e-3 and _close_enough(a, b, S):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            selected.append((sc, a))
        if len(selected) >= 3:
            break

    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_arr = None
    for _, a in scored[:5]:
        ex = _exact_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact:
            best_exact = ex
            best_arr = a.copy()

    if best_arr is None and selected:
        best_arr = selected[0][1].copy()
        best_exact = _exact_score(best_arr)

    opt_end = hard - 90.0
    if opt_end - time.monotonic() > 60.0 and selected:
        total_opt = max(0.0, opt_end - t0)
        fractions = (0.60, 0.28, 0.12)
        for i, (sc, a0) in enumerate(selected[:3]):
            if opt_end - time.monotonic() < 60.0:
                break
            frac = fractions[i] if i < len(fractions) else 0.10
            slice_time = min(frac * total_opt, opt_end - time.monotonic() - 30.0)
            if slice_time < 35.0:
                break
            deadline = time.monotonic() + slice_time
            res = _optimize(a0.copy(), deadline, rng, S, n)
            ex = _exact_score(res)
            if math.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact:
                best_exact = ex
                best_arr = res.copy()

    if best_arr is None and selected:
        best_arr = selected[0][1].copy()
    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), S)

    if hard - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
        ex = _exact_score(best_arr)
        if not math.isfinite(ex):
            best_arr = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), S)

    best_arr = _safe_nonnegative(best_arr)
    if best_arr.size != n or best_arr.sum() < 0.01:
        best_arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if hard - time.monotonic() > 10.0:
        final_ex = _exact_score(best_arr)
        if not math.isfinite(final_ex):
            best_arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(v) for v in best_arr]
```
